- **codealive**: CodeAlive context engine for semantic code search and AI-powered codebase Q&A. Enables AI coding agents to understand entire codebases beyond just open files — search across all indexed repositories, trace cross-service dependencies, discover usage patterns, and get synthesized answers to architectural questions.
